So 'Umar replied, 'Verily, he is among those whom you know.'\nThen one day he called them and invited me to sit with them, and I do not think that he invited me to be among them that day except to show them. So he said, 'What do you say about Allah's statement,\nإِذَا جَاءَ نَصْرُ اللَّهِ وَالْفَتْحُ\n(When there comes the help of Allah and the Conquest.)?'\nSome of them said, 'We were commanded to praise Allah and seek His forgiveness when He helps us and gives us victory.' Some of them remained silent and did not say anything. Then he ('Umar) said to me, 'Is this what you say, O Ibn 'Abbas?' I said, 'No.' He then said, 'What do you say?' I said, 'It was the end of the life of Allah's Messenger ﷺ that Allah was informing him of. Allah said,\nإِذَا جَاءَ نَصْرُ اللَّهِ وَالْفَتْحُ\n(When there comes the help of Allah and the Conquest.)\nwhich means, that is a sign of the end of your life.\nفَسَبِّحْ بِحَمْدِ رَبِّكَ وَاسْتَغْفِرْهُ ۚ إِنَّهُ كَانَ تَوَّابًا\n(So, glorify the praises of your Lord, and ask His forgiveness. Verily, He is the One Who accepts the repentance and Who forgives.)'\nSo, 'Umar bin Al-Khattab said, 'I do not know anything about it other than what you have said.'\" Al-Bukhari was alone in recording this Hadith.\nImam Ahmad recorded from Ibn 'Abbas that he said, \"When\nإِذَا جَاءَ نَصْرُ اللَّهِ وَالْفَتْحُ\n(When there comes the help of Allah and the Conquest.)\nwas revealed, the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nنُعِيَتْ إِلَيَّ نَفْسِي\n(My death has been announced to me.)\nAnd indeed he died during that year.\" Ahmad was alone in recording this Hadith.\nAl-Bukhari recorded that 'A'ishah said, \"The Messenger of Allah ﷺ used to say often in his bowing and prostrating,\nسُبْحَانَكَ اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّنَا وَبِحَمْدِكَ اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي\n(Glory to You, O Allah, our Lord, and praise be to You. O Allah, forgive me.)\nHe did this as his interpretation of the Qur'an (i.e., showing its implementation).\" The rest of the group has also recorded this Hadith except for At-Tirmidhi.\nImam Ahmad recorded from Masruq that 'A'ishah said, \"The Messenger of Allah ﷺ used to often say towards the end of his life,\nسُبْحَانَ اللهِ وَبِحَمْدِهِ، أَسْتَغْفِرُ اللهَ وَأَتُوبُ إِلَيْهِ\n(Glory to Allah, and praise be unto Him. I seek Allah's forgiveness and I repent to Him.)\nAnd he ﷺ said,\nإِنَّ رَبِّي كَانَ أَخْبَرَنِي أَنِّي سَأَرَى عَلَامَةً فِي أُمَّتِي، وَأَمَرَنِي إِذَا رَأَيْتُهَا أَنْ أُسَبِّحَ بِحَمْدِهِ وَأَسْتَغْفِرَهُ، إِنَّهُ كَانَ تَوَّابًا، فَقَدْ رَأَيْتُهَا:\nإِذَا جَاءَ نَصْرُ اللَّهِ وَالْفَتْحُ - وَرَأَيْتَ النَّاسَ يَدْخُلُونَ فِي دِينِ اللَّهِ أَفْوَاجًا - فَسَبِّحْ بِحَمْدِ رَبِّكَ وَاسْتَغْفِرْهُ ۚ إِنَّهُ كَانَ تَوَّابًا\n(Verily, my Lord has informed me that I will see a sign in my Ummah and He has commanded me that when I see it, I should glorify His praises and seek His forgiveness, for He is the One Who accepts repentance. And indeed I have seen it (i.e., the sign). (When there comes the help of Allah and the Conquest (Al-Fath). And you see that the people enter Allah's religion in crowds. So glorify the praises of your Lord, and ask His forgiveness. Verily, He is the One Who accepts the repentance and Who forgives.))\" Muslim also recorded this Hadith.\nThe meaning of Al-Fath here is the conquest of Makkah, and there is only one view concerning it. For indeed the different areas of the Arabs were waiting for the conquest of Makkah before they would accept Islam. They said, \"If he (Muhammad ﷺ is victorious over his people, then he is a (true) Prophet.\" So when Allah gave him victory over Makkah, they entered into the religion of Allah (Islam) in crowds. Thus, two years did not pass (after the conquest of Makkah) before the peninsula of the Arabs was laden with faith. And there did not remain any of the tribes of the Arabs except that they professed (their acceptance) of Islam. And all praise and blessings are due to Allah.\nAl-Bukhari recorded in his Sahih that 'Amr bin Salamah said, \"When Makkah was conquered, all of the people rushed to the Messenger of Allah ﷺ to profess their Islam. The various regions were delaying their acceptance of Islam until Makkah was conquered. The people used to say, 'Leave him and his people alone. If he is victorious over them he is a (true) Prophet.'\"\nWe have researched the war expedition for conquest of Makkah in our book As-Surah. Therefore, whoever wishes he may review it there. And all praise and blessings are due to Allah.\nImam Ahmad recorded from Abu 'Ammar that a neighbor of Jabir bin 'Abdullah told him, \"I returned from a journey and Jabir bin 'Abdullah came and greeted me. So I began to talk with him about the divisions among the people and what they had started doing. Thus, Jabir began to cry and he said, 'I heard the Messenger of Allah ﷺ saying,\nإِنَّ النَّاسَ دَخَلُوا فِي دِينِ اللهِ أَفْوَاجًا، وَسَيَخْرُجُونَ مِنْهُ أَفْوَاجًا\n(Verily, the people have entered into the religion of Allah in crowds and they will also leave it in crowds.)\"\nThis is the end of the Tafsir of Surat An-Nasr, and all praise and blessings are due to Allah.","href":"/tafsir/110?verse=1","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:111:1","title":"Ibn Kathir · 111:1–5","text":"بِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمَٰنِ الرَّحِيمِ\nIn the Name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful.\nتَبَّتْ يَدَا أَبِي لَهَبٍ وَتَبَّ - مَا أَغْنَىٰ عَنْهُ مَالُهُ وَمَا كَسَبَ - سَيَصْلَىٰ نَارًا ذَاتَ لَهَبٍ - وَامْرَأَتُهُ حَمَّالَةَ الْحَطَبِ - فِي جِيدِهَا حَبْلٌ مِّن مَّسَدٍ\n(1. Perish the two hands of Abu Lahab and perish he!)(2. His wealth and his children will not benefit him!)(3. He will enter a Fire full of flames!)(4. And his wife too, who carries wood.)(5. In her neck is a twisted rope of Masad.)\nThe Reason for the Revelation of this Surah and the Arrogance of Abu Lahab toward the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ)\nAl-Bukhari recorded from Ibn 'Abbas that the Prophet ﷺ went out to the valley of Al-Batha and he ascended the mountain. Then he cried out,\nيَا صَبَاحَاهُ\n(O people, come at once!) So the Quraysh gathered around him. Then he said,\nأَرَأَيْتُمْ إِنْ حَدَّثْتُكُمْ أَنَّ الْعَدُوَّ مُصَبِّحُكُمْ، أَوْ مُمَسِّيكُمْ أَكُنْتُمْ تُصَدِّقُونِّي؟\n(If I told you all that the enemy was going to attack you in the morning, or in the evening, would you all believe me?) They replied, \"Yes.\" Then he said,\nفَإِنِّي نَذِيرٌ لَكُمْ بَيْنَ يَدَيْ عَذَابٍ شَدِيدٍ\n(Verily, I am a warner (sent) to you all before the coming of a severe torment.)\nThen Abu Lahab said, \"Have you gathered us for this? May you perish!\" Thus, Allah revealed,\nتَبَّتْ يَدَا أَبِي لَهَبٍ وَتَبَّ\n(Perish the two hands of Abu Lahab and perish he!)\nto the end of the Surah.\nIn another narration it states that he stood up dusting of his hands and said, \"Perish you for the rest of this day! Have you gathered us for this?\" Then Allah revealed,\nتَبَّتْ يَدَا أَبِي لَهَبٍ وَتَبَّ\n(Perish the two hands of Abu Lahab and perish he!)\nThe first part is a supplication against him and the second is information about him. This man Abu Lahab was one of the uncles of the Messenger of Allah ﷺ.His name was 'Abdul-'Uzza bin Abdul-Muttalib. His surname was Abu 'Utaybah and he was only called Abu Lahab because of the brightness of his face. He used to often cause harm to the Messenger of Allah ﷺ. He hated and scorned him and his religion.\nImam Ahmad recorded from Abu Az-Zinad that a man called Rabi'ah bin 'Abbad from the tribe of Bani Ad-Dil, who was a man of pre-Islamic ignorance who accepted Islam, said to him, \"I saw the Prophet ﷺ in the time of pre-Islamic ignorance in the market of Dhul-Majaz and he was saying,\nيَا أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ، قُولُوا: لَا إِلهَ إِلَّا اللهُ تُفْلِحُوا\n(O people! Say there is no god worthy of worship except Allah and you will be successful.)\nThe people were gathered around him and behind him there was a man with a bright face, squint (or cross) eyes and two braids in his hair. He was saying, \"Verily, he is an apostate (from our religion) and a liar!\" This man was following him (the Prophet ﷺ) around wherever he went. So, I asked who was he and they (the people) said, \"This is his uncle, Abu Lahab.\"\nAhmad also recorded this narration from Surayj, who reported it from Ibn Abu Az-Zinad, who reported it from his father (Abu Zinad) who mentioned this same narration. However in this report, Abu Zinad said, \"I said to Rabi'ah, 'Were you a child at that time' He replied, 'No. By Allah, that day I was most intelligent, and I was the strongest blower of the flute (for music).'\" Ahmad was alone in recording this Hadith.\nConcerning Allah's statement,\nمَا أَغْنَىٰ عَنْهُ مَالُهُ وَمَا كَسَبَ\n(His wealth and his children (Kasab) will not benefit him!)\nIbn 'Abbas and others have said,\nوَمَا كَسَبَ\n(and his children (Kasab) will not benefit him!)\n\"Kasab means his children.\" A similar statement has been reported from 'A'ishah, Mujahid, 'Ata', Al-Hasan and Ibn Sirin.\nIt has been mentioned from Ibn Mas'ud that when the Messenger of Allah ﷺ called his people to faith, Abu Lahab said, \"Even if what my nephew says is true, I will ransom myself (i.e., save myself) from the painful torment on the Day of Judgement with my wealth and my children.\" Thus, Allah revealed,\nمَا أَغْنَىٰ عَنْهُ مَالُهُ وَمَا كَسَبَ\n(and his children (Kasab) will not benefit him!)\nThen Allah says,\nسَيَصْلَىٰ نَارًا ذَاتَ لَهَبٍ\n(He will enter a Fire full of flames!) meaning, it has flames, evil and severe burning.\nThe Destiny of Umm Jamil, the Wife of Abu Lahab\nوَامْرَأَتُهُ حَمَّالَةَ الْحَطَبِ\n(And his wife too, who carries wood.) His wife was among the leading women of the Quraysh and she was known as Umm Jamil. Her name was 'Arwah bint Harb bin Umayyah and she was the sister of Abu Sufyan. She was supportive of her husband in his disbelief, rejection and obstinacy. Therefore, she will be helping to administer his punishment in the fire of Hell on the Day of Judgement. Thus, Allah says,\nحَمَّالَةَ الْحَطَبِ - فِي جِيدِهَا حَبْلٌ مِّن مَّسَدٍ\n(Who carries wood. In her neck is a twisted rope of Masad.)\nmeaning, she will carry the firewood and throw it upon her husband to increase that which he is in (of torment), and she will be ready and prepared to do so.\nفِي جِيدِهَا حَبْلٌ مِّن مَّسَدٍ\n(In her neck is a twisted rope of Masad.)\nMujahid and 'Urwah both said, \"From the palm fiber of the Fire.\"\nAl-'Awfi narrated from Ibn 'Abbas, 'Atiyah Al-Jadali, Ad-Dahhak and Ibn Zayd that she used to place thorns in the path of the Messenger of Allah ﷺ. Al-Jawhari said, \"Al-Masad refers to fibers, it is also a rope made from fibers or palm leaves. It is also made from the skins of camels or their furs. It is said (in Arabic) Masadtul-Habla and Amsaduhu Masadan, when you tightly fasten its twine.\"\nMujahid said,\nفِي جِيدِهَا حَبْلٌ مِّن مَّسَدٍ\n(In her neck is a twisted rope of Masad.) \"This means a collar of iron.\" Don't you see that the Arabs call a pulley cable a Masad?\nA Story of Abu Lahab's Wife harming the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ)\nIbn Abi Hatim said that his father and Abu Zur'ah both said that 'Abdullah bin Az-Zubayr Al-Humaydi told them that Sufyan informed them that Al-Walid bin Kathir related from Ibn Tadrus who reported that Asma' bint Abi Bakr said, \"When\nتَبَّتْ يَدَا أَبِي لَهَبٍ\n(Perish the two hands of Abu Lahab and perish he)!)\nwas revealed, the one-eyed Umm Jamil bint Harb came out wailing, and she had a stone in her hand. She was saying, 'He criticizes our father, and his religion is our scorn, and his command is to disobey us.' The Messenger of Allah ﷺ was sitting in the Masjid (of the Ka'bah) and Abu Bakr was with him. When Abu Bakr saw her he said, 'O Messenger of Allah! She is coming and I fear that she will see you.' The Messenger of Allah ﷺ replied,\nإِنَّهَا لَنْ تَرَانِي\n(Verily, she will not see me.)\nThen he recited some of the Qur'an as a protection for himself. This is as Allah says,\nوَإِذَا قَرَأْتَ الْقُرْآنَ جَعَلْنَا بَيْنَكَ وَبَيْنَ الَّذِينَ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ بِالْآخِرَةِ حِجَابًا مَّسْتُورًا\n(And when you recite the Qur'an, We put between you and those who believe not in the Hereafter, an invisible veil.)(17:45)\nSo she advanced until she was standing in front of Abu Bakr and she did not see the Messenger of Allah ﷺ. She then said, 'O Abu Bakr! Verily, I have been informed that your friend is making defamatory poetry about me.' Abu Bakr replied, 'Nay! By the Lord of this House (the Ka'bah) he is not defaming you.' So she turned away saying, 'Indeed the Quraysh know that I am the daughter of their leader.'\"\nAl-Walid or another person said in a different version of this Hadith, \"So Umm Jamil stumbled over her waist gown while she was making circuits (Tawaf) around the House (the Ka'bah) and she said, 'Cursed be the reviler.' Then Umm Hakim bint 'Abdul-Muttalib said, 'I am a chaste woman so I will not speak abusively and I am refined so I do not know. Both of us are children of the same uncle. And after all the Quraysh know best.\"\nThis is the end of the Tafsir of this Surah, and all praise and blessings are due to Allah.","href":"/tafsir/111?verse=1","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:112:1","title":"Ibn Kathir · 112:1–4","text":"The Reason for the Revelation of this Surah and its Virtues\nImam Ahmad recorded from Ubayy bin Ka'b that the idolators said to the Prophet ﷺ, \"O Muhammad! Tell us the lineage of your Lord.\" So Allah revealed\nقُلْ هُوَ اللَّهُ أَحَدٌ - اللَّهُ الصَّمَدُ - لَمْ يَلِدْ وَلَمْ يُولَدْ - وَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُ كُفُوًا أَحَدٌ\n(1. Say: \"He is Allah\")(2. \"One\".)(3. \"He begets not, nor was He begotten\".)(4. \"And there is non comparable to Him.\")\nSimilar was recorded by At-Tirmidhi and Ibn Jarir and they added in their narration that he said,\nالصَّمَدُ\n(As-Samad) is One Who does not give birth, nor was He born, because there is nothing that is born except that it will die, and there is nothing that dies except that it leaves behind inheritance, and indeed Allah does not die and He does not leave behind any inheritance.\nوَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُ كُفُوًا أَحَدٌ\n(And there is none comparable to Him.)\nThis means that there is none similar to Him, none equal to Him and there is nothing at all like Him.\"\nIbn Abi Hatim also recorded it and At-Tirmidhi mentioned it as a Mursal narration. Then At-Tirmidhi said, \"And this is the most correct.\"\nA Hadith on its Virtues\nAl-Bukhari reported from 'Amrah bint 'Abdur-Rahman, who used to stay in the apartment of 'A'ishah, the wife of the Prophet ﷺ, that 'A'ishah said, \"The Prophet ﷺ sent a man as the commander of a war expedition and he used to lead his companions in prayer with recitation (of the Qur'an). And he would complete his recitation with the recitation of 'Say: He is Allah, One.' So when they returned they mentioned that to the Prophet ﷺ and he said,\nسَلُوهُ لِأَيِّ شَيْءٍ يَصْنَعُ ذَلِكَ؟\n(Ask him why does he do that.)\nSo they asked him and he said, 'Because it is the description of Ar-Rahman and I love to recite it. So the Prophet ﷺ said,\nأَخْبِرُوهُ أَنَّ اللهَ تَعَالَى يُحِبُّهُ\n(Inform him that Allah the Most High loves him.)\"\nThis is how Al-Bukhari recorded this Hadith in his Book of Tawhid. Muslim and An-Nasa'i also recorded it.\nIn his Book of Salah, Al-Bukhari recorded that Anas said, \"A man from the Ansar used to lead the people in prayer in the Masjid of Quba'. Whenever he began a Surah in the recitation of the prayer that he was leading them, he would start by reciting 'Say: He is Allah, One' until he completed the entire Surah.\nThen he would recite another Surah along with it (after it). And used to do this in every Rak'ah. So his companions spoke to him about this saying; 'Verily, you begin the prayer with this Surah. Then you think that it is not sufficient for you unless you recite another Surah as well. So you should either recite it or leave it and recite another Surah instead.'\nThe man replied, 'I will not leave it off. If you want me to continue leading you (in prayer), I will do this; and if you all do not like it, I will leave you (i.e., I will stop leading you).' They used to consider him to be of the best of them to lead them in prayer and they did not want anyone else to lead them other than him. So, when the Prophet ﷺ came they informed him of this information and he said,\nيَا فُلَانُ، مَا يَمْنَعُكَ أَنْ تَفْعَلَ مَا يَأْمُرُكَ بِهِ أَصْحَابُكَ، وَمَا حَمَلَكَ عَلَى لُزُومِ هَذِهِ السُّورَةِ فِي كُلِّ رَكْعَةٍ؟\n(O so-and-so! What prevents you from doing what your companions are commanding you to do, and what makes you adhere to the recitation of this Surah in every Rak'ah?)\nThe man said, 'Verily, I love it.' The Prophet ﷺ replied,\nحُبُّكَ إِيَّاهَا أَدْخَلَكَ الْجَنَّةَ\n(Your love of it will cause you to enter Paradise.) This was recorded by Al-Bukhari, with a disconnected chain, but in a manner indicating his approval.\nA Hadith that mentions this Surah is equivalent to a Third of the Qur'an\nAl-Bukhari recorded from Abu Sa'id that a man heard another man reciting\nقُلْ هُوَ اللَّهُ أَحَدٌ\n(Say: \"He is Allah, One.\") and he was repeating over and over. So when morning came, the man went to the Prophet ﷺ and mentioned that to him, and it was as though he was belittling it. The Prophet ﷺ said,\nوَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ إِنَّهَا لَتَعْدِلُ ثُلُثَ الْقُرْآنِ\n(By He in Whose Hand is my soul, verily it is equivalent to a third of the Qur'an.) Abu Dawud and An-Nasa'i also recorded it.\nAnother Hadith\nAl-Bukhari recorded from Abu Sa'id, may Allah be pleased with him, that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said to his Companions,\nأَيَعْجِزُ أَحَدُكُمْ أَنْ يَقْرَأَ ثُلُثَ الْقُرْآنِ فِي لَيْلَةٍ؟\n(Is one of you not able to recite a third of the Qur'an in a single night?)\nThis was something that was difficult for them and they said, \"Which of us is able to do that, O Messenger of Allah?\" So he ﷺ replied,\nاللهُ الْوَاحِدُ الصَّمَدُ ثُلُثُ الْقُرْآنِ\n(\"Allah is the One, As-Samad\" is a third of the Qur'an.)\nAl-Bukhari was alone in recording this Hadith.\nAnother Hadith that its Recitation Necessitates Admission into Paradise\nImam Malik bin Anas recorded from 'Ubayd bin Hunayn that he heard Abu Hurayrah saying, \"I went out with the Prophet ﷺ and he heard a man reciting 'Say: He is Allah, the One.' So the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nوَجَبَت\n(It is obligatory.) I asked, 'What is obligatory?' He replied,\nالْجَنَّةُ\n(Paradise.)\"\nAt-Tirmidhi and An-Nasa'i also recorded it by way of Malik, and At-Tirmidhi said, \"Hasan Sahih Gharib. We do not know of it except as a narration of Malik.\"\nThe Hadith in which the Prophet ﷺ said,\nحُبُّكَ إِيَّاهَا أَدْخَلَكَ الْجَنَّةَ\n(Your love of it will cause you to enter Paradise.) has already been mentioned.\nA Hadith about repeating this Surah\nAbdullah bin Imam Ahmad recorded from Mu'adh bin 'Abdullah bin Khubayb, who reported that his father said, \"We became thirsty and it had become dark while we were waiting for the Messenger of Allah ﷺ to lead us in prayer. Then, when he came out he took me by my hand and said,\nقُلْ\n(Say.) Then he was silent. Then he said again,\nقُلْ\n(Say.) So I said, 'What should I say?' He said,\nقُلْ هُوَ اللَّهُ أَحَدٌ\nوَالْمُعَوِّذَتَيْنِ حِينَ تُمْسِي وَحِينَ تُصْبِحُ ثَلَاثًا، تَكْفِكَ كُلَّ يَوْمٍ مَرَّتَيْنِ\n(Say: \"He is Allah, One,\" and the two Surahs of Refuge (Al-Falaq and An-Nas) when you enter upon the evening and the morning three times (each). They will be sufficient for you two times every day.)\"\nThis Hadith was also recorded by Abu Dawud, At-Tirmidhi and An-Nasa'i. At-Tirmidhi said, \"Hasan Sahih Gharib.\" An-Nasa'i also recorded through another chain of narrators with the wording,\nيَكْفِكَ كُلَّ شَيْءٍ\n(They will suffice you against everything.)\nAnother Hadith about supplicating with it by Allah's Names\nIn his Book of Tafsir, An-Nasa'i recorded from 'Abdullah bin Buraydah, who reported from his father that he entered the Masjid with the Messenger of Allah ﷺ, and there was a man praying and supplicating saying, \"O Allah! Verily, I ask you by my testifying that there is no God worthy of worship except You. You are the One, the Self-Sufficient Sustainer of all, Who does not give birth, nor were You born, and there is none comparable to Him.\" The Prophet ﷺ said,\nوَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لَقَدْ سَأَلَهُ بِاسْمِهِ الْأَعْظَمِ، الَّذِي إِذَا سُئِلَ بِهِ أَعطَى، وإِذَا دُعِيَ بِهِ أَجَابَ\n(By He in Whose Hand is my soul, verily he has asked Him by His Greatest Name. It is His Name that if He is asked by it, He will give, and if He is called upon by it, He will answer.)\nThe other Sunan compilers have also recorded this Hadith. At-Tirmidhi said, \"Hasan Gharib.\"\nA Hadith about seeking a Cure by these Surahs\nAl-Bukhari recorded from 'A'ishah that whenever the Prophet ﷺ would go to bed every night, he would put his palms together and blow into them. Then he would recite into them (his palms), 'Say: He is Allah, One', 'Say: I seek refuge with the Lord of Al-Falaq', and 'Say: I seek refuge with the Lord of mankind.' Then he would wipe whatever he was able to of his body with them (his palms). He would begin wiping his head and face with them and the front part of his body. He would do this (wiping his body) three times. The Sunan compilers also recorded this same Hadith.\nبِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمَٰنِ الرَّحِيمِ\nIn the Name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful.\nقُلْ هُوَ اللَّهُ أَحَدٌ - اللَّهُ الصَّمَدُ - لَمْ يَلِدْ وَلَمْ يُولَدْ - وَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُ كُفُوًا أَحَدٌ\n(1. Say: \"He is Allah, One.\")(2. \"Allah As-Samad.\")(3. \"He begets not, nor was He begotten.\")(4. \"And there is none comparable to Him.\")\nThe reason for the revelation of this Surah has already been mentioned. 'Ikrimah said, \"When the Jews said, 'We worship 'Uzayr, the son of Allah,' and the Christians said, 'We worship the Messiah ('Isa), the son of Allah,' and the Zoroastrians said, 'We worship the sun and the moon,' and the idolators said, 'We worship idols,' Allah revealed to His Messenger ﷺ,\nقُلْ هُوَ اللَّهُ أَحَدٌ\n(Say: \"He is Allah, One.\") meaning, He is the One, the Singular, Who has no peer, no assistant, no rival, no equal and none comparable to Him. This word (Al-Ahad) cannot be used for anyone in affirmation except Allah the Mighty and Majestic, because He is perfect in all of His attributes and actions. Concerning His saying,\nاللَّهُ الصَّمَدُ\n(Allah As-Samad.) 'Ikrimah reported that Ibn 'Abbas said, \"This means the One Who all of the creation depends upon for their needs and their requests.\" 'Ali bin Abi Talhah reported from Ibn 'Abbas, \"He is the Master Who is perfect in His sovereignty, the Most Noble Who is perfect in His nobility, the Most Magnificent Who is perfect in His magnificence, the Most Forbearing Who is perfect in His forbearance, the All-Knowing Who is perfect in His knowledge, and the Most Wise Who is perfect in His wisdom. He is the One Who is perfect in all aspects of nobility and authority. He is Allah, glory be unto Him. These attributes are not befitting anyone other than Him. He has no coequal and nothing is like Him. Glory be to Allah, the One, the Irresistible.\" Al-A'mash reported from Shaqiq, who said that Abu Wa'il said,\nالصَّمَدُ\n(As-Samad.) is the Master Whose control is complete.\"\nAllah is Above having Children and procreating\nThen Allah says,\nلَمْ يَلِدْ وَلَمْ يُولَدْ - وَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُ كُفُوًا أَحَدٌ\n(He begets not, nor was He begotten. And there is none comparable to Him.)\nmeaning, He does not have any child, parent or spouse. Mujahid said,\nوَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُ كُفُوًا أَحَدٌ\n(And there is none comparable to Him.) \"This means He does not have a spouse.\" This is as Allah says,\nبَدِيعُ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ ۖ أَنَّىٰ يَكُونُ لَهُ وَلَدٌ وَلَمْ تَكُن لَّهُ صَاحِبَةٌ ۖ وَخَلَقَ كُلَّ شَيْءٍ\n(He is the Originator of the heavens and the earth. How can He have children when He has no wife He created all things.)(6:101)\nmeaning, He owns everything and He created everything. So how can He have a peer among His creatures who can be equal to Him, or a relative who can resemble Him? Glorified, Exalted and far removed is Allah from such a thing.\nAllah says,\nوَقَالُوا اتَّخَذَ الرَّحْمَٰنُ وَلَدًا - لَّقَدْ جِئْتُمْ شَيْئًا إِدًّا - تَكَادُ السَّمَاوَاتُ يَتَفَطَّرْنَ مِنْهُ وَتَنشَقُّ الْأَرْضُ وَتَخِرُّ الْجِبَالُ هَدًّا - أَن دَعَوْا لِلرَّحْمَٰنِ وَلَدًا - وَمَا يَنبَغِي لِلرَّحْمَٰنِ أَن يَتَّخِذَ وَلَدًا - إِن كُلُّ مَن فِي السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ إِلَّا آتِي الرَّحْمَٰنِ عَبْدًا - لَّقَدْ أَحْصَاهُمْ وَعَدَّهُمْ عَدًّا - وَكُلُّهُمْ آتِيهِ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ فَرْدًا\n(And they say: Ar-Rahman has begotten a son. Indeed you have brought forth (said) a terrible evil thing. Whereby the heavens are almost torn, and the earth is split asunder, and the mountains fall in ruins, that they ascribe a son to Ar-Rahman. But it is not suitable for Ar-Rahman that He should beget a son. There is none in the heavens and the earth but comes unto Ar-Rahman as a slave. Verily, He knows each one of them, and has counted them a full counting. And all of them will come to Him alone on the Day of Resurrection.)(19:88-95)\nAnd Allah says,\nوَقَالُوا اتَّخَذَ الرَّحْمَٰنُ وَلَدًا ۗ سُبْحَانَهُ ۚ بَلْ عِبَادٌ مُّكْرَمُونَ - لَا يَسْبِقُونَهُ بِالْقَوْلِ وَهُم بِأَمْرِهِ يَعْمَلُونَ\n(And they say: \"Ar-Rahman has begotten a son. Glory to Him! They are but honored servants. They speak not until He has spoken, and they act on His command.)(21:26-27)\nAllah also says,\nوَجَعَلُوا بَيْنَهُ وَبَيْنَ الْجِنَّةِ نَسَبًا ۚ وَلَقَدْ عَلِمَتِ الْجِنَّةُ إِنَّهُمْ لَمُحْضَرُونَ - سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ عَمَّا يَصِفُونَ\n(And they have invented a kinship between Him and the Jinn, but the Jinn know well that they have indeed to appear before Him. Glorified is Allah! (He is free) from what they attribute unto Him!)(37:158-159)\nIn Sahih Al-Bukhari, it is recorded (that that the Prophet ﷺ said),\nلَا أَحَدَ أَصْبَرُ عَلَى أَذًى سَمِعَهُ مِنَ اللهِ، يَجْعَلُونَ لَهُ وَلَدًا، وَهُوَ يَرْزُقُهُمْ وَيُعَافِيهِم\n(There is no one more patient with something harmful that he hears than Allah. They attribute a son to Him, while it is He Who gives them sustenance and cures them.)\nAl-Bukhari also recorded from Abu Hurayrah that the Prophet ﷺ said,\nقَالَ اللهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ: كَذَّبَنِي ابْنُ آدَمَ وَلَمْ يَكُنْ لَهُ ذَلِكَ، وَشَتَمَنِي وَلَمْ يَكُنْ لَهُ ذَلِكَ، فَأَمَّا تَكْذِيبُهُ إِيَّايَ فَقَوْلُهُ: لَنْ يُعِيدَنِي كَمَا بَدَأَنِي، وَلَيْسَ أَوَّلُ الْخَلْقِ بِأَهْوَنَ عَلَيَّ مِنْ إِعَادَتِهِ، وَأَمَّا شَتْمُهُ إِيَّايَ فَقَوْلُهُ: اتَّخَذَ اللهُ وَلَدًا، وَأَنَا الْأَحَدُ الصَّمَدُ، لَمْ أَلِدْ وَلَمْ أُولَدْ، وَلَمْ يَكُنْ لِي كُفُوًا أَحَدٌ\n(Allah the Mighty and Majestic says, \"The Son of Adam denies Me and he has no right to do so, and he abuses Me and he has no right to do so. In reference to his denial of Me, it is his saying: 'He (Allah) will never re-create me like He created me before.' But the re-creation of him is easier than his original creation. As for his cursing Me, it is his saying: 'Allah has taken a son.' But I am the One, the Self-Sufficient Master. I do not give birth, nor was I born, and there is none comparable to Me.\")\nThis is the end of the Tafsir of Surat Al-Ikhlas, and all praise and blessings are due to Allah.","href":"/tafsir/112?verse=1","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:113:1","title":"Ibn Kathir · 113:1–5","text":"The Position of Ibn Mas'ud concerning Al-Mu'awwidhatayn\nImam Ahmad recorded from Zirr bin Hubaysh that Ubayy bin Ka'b told him that Ibn Mas'ud did not record the Mu'awwidhatayn in his Mushaf (copy of the Qur'an). So Ubayy said, \"I testify that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ informed me that Jibril said to him,\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of Al-Falaq.\")(113:1)\nSo he said it. And Jibril said to him,\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of mankind.\")(114:1)\nSo he said it. Therefore, we say what the Prophet ﷺ said.\"\nThe Virtues of Surahs Al-Falaq and An-Nas\nIn his Sahih, Muslim recorded on the authority of 'Uqbah bin 'Amir that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nأَلَمْ تَرَ آيَاتٍ أُنْزِلَتْ هَذِهِ اللَّيْلَةَ لَمْ يُرَ مِثْلُهُنَّ قَطُّ:\n(Do you not see that there have been Ayat revealed to me tonight the like of which has not been seen before?) They are\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with, the Lord of Al-Falaq.\")(113:1)\nand;\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of mankind.\")(114:1))\nThis Hadith was recorded by Ahmad, At-Tirmidhi and An-Nasa'i. At-Tirmidhi said, \"Hasan Sahih.\"\nAnother Narration\nImam Ahmad recorded from 'Uqbah bin 'Amir that he said, \"While I was leading the Messenger of Allah ﷺ along one of these paths he said,\nيَا عُقْبَةُ أَلَا تَرْكَبُ؟\n(O 'Uqbah! Will you not ride?)\nI was afraid that this might be considered an act of disobedience. So the Messenger of Allah ﷺ got down and I rode for a while. Then he rode. Then he ﷺ said,\nيَا عُقْبَةُ، أَلَا أُعَلِّمُكَ سُورَتَيْنِ مِنْ خَيْرِ سُورَتَيْنِ قَرَأَ بِهِمَا النَّاسُ؟\n(O 'Uqbah! Should I not teach you two Surahs that are of the best two Surahs that the people recite?)\nI said, 'Of course, O Messenger of Allah.' So he taught me to recite\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with, the Lord of Al-Falaq.\")(113:1)\nand\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of mankind.\")(114:1)\nThen the call was given to begin the prayer and the Messenger of Allah ﷺ went forward (to lead the people), and he recited them in the prayer. Afterwards he passed by me and said,\nكَيْفَ رَأَيْتَ يَا عُقَيْبُ، اقْرَأْ بِهِمَا كُلَّمَا نِمْتَ وَكُلَّمَا قُمْتَ\n(What do you think, O 'Uqayb? Recite these two Surahs whenever you go to sleep and whenever you get up.)\"\nAn-Nasa'i and Abu Dawud both recorded this Hadith.\nAnother Narration\nAn-Nasa'I recorded from 'Uqbah bin 'Amir that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nإِنَّ النَّاسَ لَمْ يَتَعَوَّذُوا بِمِثْلِ هَذَيْنِ:\n(Verily, the people do not seek protection with anything like these two:\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with, the Lord of Al-Falaq.\")(113:1)\nand;\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of mankind.\")(114:1)\nAnother Narration\nAn-Nasa'i recorded that 'Uqbah bin 'Amir said, \"I was walking with the Messenger of Allah ﷺ when he said,\nيَا عُقْبَةُ قُلْ\n(O 'Uqbah! Say!) I replied, 'What should I say?' So he was silent and did not respond to me. Then he ﷺ said,\nقُلْ\n(Say!) I replied, 'What should I say, O Messenger of Allah?' He said,\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of Al-Falaq.\")\nSo, I recited it until I reached its end. Then he said,\nقُلْ\n(Say!) I replied, 'What should I say O Messenger of Allah?' He said,\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of mankind.\")\nSo, I recited it until I reached its end. Then the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nمَا سَأَلَ سَائِلٌ بِمِثْلِهَا، وَلَا اسْتَعَاذَ مُسْتَعِيذٌ بِمِثْلِهَا\n(No person beseeches with anything like these, and no person seeks refuge with anything like these.)\"\nAnother Hadith\nAn-Nasa'i recorded that Ibn 'Abis Al-Juhani said that the Prophet ﷺ said to him,\nيَا ابْنَ عَابِسٍ أَلَا أَدُلُّكَ - أَوْ أَلَا أُخْبِرُكَ - بِأَفْضَلِ مَا يَتَعَوَّذُ بِهِ الْمُتَعَوِّذُونَ؟\n(O Ibn 'Abis! Shall I guide you to – or inform you – of the best thing that those who seek protection use for protection?)\nHe replied, \"Of course, O Messenger of Allah!\" The Prophet ﷺ said,\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ\nوَ\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ النَّاسِ\nهَاتَانِ السُّورَتَانِ\n(Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of Al-Falaq.\") and (Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of mankind.\") These two Surahs (are the best protection).\nImam Malik recorded from 'A'ishah that whenever the Messenger of Allah ﷺ was suffering from an ailment, he would recite the Mu'awwidhatayn over himself and blow (over himself). Then if his pain became severe, 'A'ishah said that she would recite the Mu'awwidhatayn over him and take his hand and wipe it over himself seeking the blessing of those Surahs. Al-Bukhari, Abu Dawud, An-Nasa'i and Ibn Majah all recorded this Hadith.\nIt has been reported from Abu Sa'id that the Messenger of Allah ﷺ used to seek protection against the evil eyes of the Jinns and mankind. But when the Mu'awwi- dhatayn were revealed, he used them (for protection) and abandoned all else besides them. At-Tirmidhi, An-Nasa'i and Ibn Majah recorded this. At-Tirmidhi said, \"This Hadith is Hasan Sahih.\"\nبِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمَٰنِ الرَّحِيمِ\nIn the Name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful.\nقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِرَبِّ الْفَلَقِ - مِن شَرِّ مَا خَلَقَ - وَمِن شَرِّ غَاسِقٍ إِذَا وَقَبَ - وَمِن شَرِّ النَّفَّاثَاتِ فِي الْعُقَدِ - وَمِن شَرِّ حَاسِدٍ إِذَا حَسَدَ\n(1. Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of Al-Falaq,\")(2. \"From the evil of what He has created,\")(3. \"And from the evil of the Ghasiq when Waqab,\")(4. \"And from the evil of the blowers in knots,\")(5. \"And from the evil of the envier when he envies.\")\nIbn Abi Hatim recorded that Jabir said, \"Al-Falaq is the morning.\" Al-'Awfi reported from Ibn 'Abbas, \"Al-Falaq is the morning.\" The same has been reported from Mujahid, Sa'id bin Jubayr, 'Abdullah bin Muhammad bin 'Aqil, Al-Hasan, Qatadah, Muhammad bin Ka'b Al-Qurazi and Ibn Zayd. Malik also reported a similar statement from Zayd bin Aslam. Al-Qurazi, Ibn Zayd and Ibn Jarir all said, \"This is like Allah's saying,\nفَالِقُ الْإِصْبَاحِ\n(He is the Cleaver of the daybreak.).\" (6:96)\nAllah said,\nمِن شَرِّ مَا خَلَقَ\n(From the evil of what He has created,)\nThis means from the evil of all created things. Thabit Al-Bunani and Al-Hasan Al-Basri both said, \"Hell, Iblis and his progeny, from among that which He (Allah) created.\"\nوَمِن شَرِّ غَاسِقٍ إِذَا وَقَبَ\n(And from the evil of the Ghasiq when Waqab,)\nMujahid said, \"Ghasiq is the night, and 'when it Waqab' refers to the setting of the sun.\" Al-Bukhari mentioned this from him. Ibn Abi Najih also reported a similar narration from him (Mujahid).\nThe same was said by Ibn 'Abbas, Muhammad bin Ka'b Al-Qurazi, Ad-Dahhak, Khusayf, Al-Hasan and Qatadah. They said, \"Verily, it is the night when it advances with its darkness.\" Az-Zuhri said,\nوَمِن شَرِّ غَاسِقٍ إِذَا وَقَبَ\n(And from the evil of the Ghasiq when Waqab,)\n\"This means the sun when it sets.\" Abu Al-Muhazzim reported that Abu Hurayrah said,\nوَمِن شَرِّ غَاسِقٍ إِذَا وَقَبَ\n(And from the evil of the Ghasiq when Waqab,)\n\"This means the star.\" Ibn Zayd said, \"The Arabs used to say, 'Al-Ghasiq is the declination (of the position) of the heavenly body known as Pleiades. The number of those who were ill and stricken with plague would increase whenever it would decline, and their number would lessen whenever it rose.'\"\nIbn Jarir said, \"Others have said that it is the moon.\"\nThe support for the people who hold this position (that it means the moon) is a narration that Imam Ahmad recorded from Al-Harith bin Abi Salamah. He said that 'A'ishah said, \"The Messenger of Allah ﷺ took me by my hand and showed me the moon when it rose, and he said,\nتَعَوَّذِي بِاللهِ مِنْ شَرِّ هَذَا الْغَاسِقِ إِذَا وَقَبَ\n(Seek refuge with Allah from the evil of this Ghasiq when it becomes dark.)\"\nAt-Tirmidhi and An-Nasa'i both recorded this Hadith in their Books of Tafsir in their Sunans.\nAllah said,\nوَمِن شَرِّ النَّفَّاثَاتِ فِي الْعُقَدِ\n(And from the evil of the blowers in knots,)\nMujahid, 'Ikrimah, Al-Hasan, Qatadah and Ad-Dahhak all said, \"This means the witches.\" Mujahid said, \"When they perform their spells and blow into the knots.\"\nIn another Hadith it has been reported that Jibril came to the Prophet ﷺ and said, \"Are you suffering from any ailment, O Muhammad?\" The Prophet ﷺ replied,\nنَعَمْ\n(Yes.) So Jibril said, \"In the Name of Allah, I recite prayer (Ruqyah) over you, from every illness that harms you, from the evil of every envious person and evil eye. May Allah cure you.\"\nDiscussion of the Bewitchment of the Prophet (ﷺ)\nIn the Book of Medicine of his Sahih, Al-Bukhari recorded that 'A'ishah said, \"The Messenger of Allah ﷺ was bewitched until he thought that he had relations with his wives, but he had not had relations with them.\" Sufyan said, \"This is the worst form of magic when it reaches this stage.\" So the Prophet ﷺ said,\nيَا عَائِشَةُ، أَعَلِمْتِ أَنَّ اللهَ قَدْ أَفْتَانِي فِيمَا اسْتَفْتَيْتُهُ فِيهِ؟ أَتَانِي رَجُلَانِ فَقَعَدَ أَحَدُهُمَا عِنْدَ رَأْسِي وَالْآخَرُ عِنْدَ رِجْلَيَّ، فَقَال الَّذِي عِنْدَ رَأْسِي لِلْآخَرِ: مَا بَالُ الرَّجُلِ؟ قَالَ: مَطْبُوبٌ، قَالَ: وَمَنْ طَبَّهُ، قَالَ: لَبِيدُ بْنُ أَعْصَمَ: رَجُلٌ مِنْ بَنِي زُرَيْقٍ حَلِيفٌ لِيَهُودَ، كَانَ مُنَافِقًا، قَالَ: وَفِيمَ؟ قَالَ: فِي مُشْطٍ وَمُشَاطَةٍ، قَالَ: وَأَيْنَ؟ قَالَ: فِي جُفِّ طَلْعَةٍ ذَكَرٍ، تَحْتَ رَاعُوفَةٍ فِي بِئْرِ ذَرْوَانَ\n(O 'A'ishah! Do you know that Allah has answered me concerning that which I asked Him? Two men came to me and one of them sat by my head while the other sat by my feet. The one who was sitting by my head said to the other one, 'What is wrong with this man?' The other replied, 'He is bewitched.' The first one said, 'Who bewitched him?' The other replied, 'Labid bin A'sam. He is a man from the tribe of Banu Zurayq who is an ally of the Jews, and a hypocrite.' The first one asked, 'With what (did he bewitch him)?' The other replied, 'With a comb and hair from the comb.' The first one asked, 'Where (is the comb)?' The other answered, 'In the dried bark of a male date palm under a rock in a well called Dharwan.')\nA'ishah said, \"So he went to the well to remove it (the comb with the hair). Then he said,\nهَذِهِ الْبِئْرُ الَّتِي أُرِيتُهَا، وَكَأَنَّ مَاءَهَا نُقَاعَةُ الحِنَّاءِ، وَكَأَنَّ نَخْلَهَا رُؤُوسُ الشَّيَاطِين\n(This is the well that I saw. It was as if its water had henna soaked in it and its palm trees were like the heads of devils.)\nSo he removed it (of the well). Then I ('A'ishah) said, 'Will you not make this public?' He replied,\nأَمَّا اللهُ فَقَدْ شَفَانِي، وَأَكْرَهُ أَنْ أُثِيرَ عَلَى أَحَدٍ مِنَ النَّاسِ شَرًّا\n(Allah has cured me and I hate to spread (the news of) wickedness to any of the people.)\"","href":"/tafsir/113?verse=1","kind":"tafsir"},{"id":"tafsir:114:1","title":"Ibn Kathir · 114:1–6","text":"Say: \"I seek refuge with the Lord of An-Nas, (1)\"The King of An-Nas, (2)\"The God of An-Nas, (3)\"From the evil of the whisperer who withdraws. (4)\"Who whispers in the breasts of An-Nas. (5)\"Of Jinn and An-Nas. (6)\nThese are three attributes from the attributes of the Lord, the Mighty and Majestic. They are lordship, sovereignty and divinity. Thus, He is the Lord of everything, the King of everything and the God of everything. All things are created by Him, owned by Him, and subservient to Him. Therefore, He commands whoever is seeking protection to seek refuge with the One Who has these attributes from the evil of the whisperer who withdraws. This (the whisperer) is the devil that is assigned to man. For verily, there is not any of the Children of Adam except that he has a companion that beautifies wicked deeds for him. This devil will go to any lengths to confuse and confound him. The only person who is safe is He Whom Allah protects.\nIt is confirmed in the Sahih that he (the Prophet ﷺ) said,\nمَا مِنْكُمْ مِنْ أَحَدٍ إِلَّا قَدْ وُكِّلَ بِهِ قَرِينُهُ\n(There is not a single one of you except that his companion (a devil) has been assigned to him.) They (the Companions) said, \"What about you, O Messenger of Allah?\" He replied,\nنَعَمْ، إِلَّا أَنَّ اللهَ أَعَانَنِي عَلَيْهِ فَأَسْلَمَ، فَلَا يَأْمُرُنِي إِلَّا بِخَيْرٍ\n(Yes. However, Allah has helped me against him and he has accepted Islam. Thus, he only commands me to do good.)\nIt is also confirmed in the Two Sahihs from Anas, who reported the story of Safiyyah when she came to visit the Prophet ﷺ while he was performing I'tikaf, that he went out with her during the night to walk her back to her house. So, two men from the Ansar met him (on the way). When they saw the Prophet ﷺ, they began walking swiftly. So, the Messenger of Allah ﷺ said,\nعَلَى رِسْلِكُمَا، إِنَّهَا صَفِيَّةُ بِنْتُ حُيَيٍّ\n(Slow down! This is Safiyyah bint Huyay!)\nThey said, \"Glory be to Allah, O Messenger of Allah!\" He said,\nإِنَّ الشَّيْطَانَ يَجْرِي مِنِ ابْنِ آدَمَ مَجْرَى الدَّمِ، وَإِنِّي خَشِيتُ أَنْ يَقْذِفَ فِي قُلُوبِكُمَا شَيْئًا، أَوْ قَالَ: شَرًّا\n(Verily, Shaytan runs in the Son of Adam like the running of the blood. And verily, I feared that he might cast something into your hearts – or he said – evil.)\nSa'id bin Jubayr reported that Ibn 'Abbas said concerning Allah's statement,\nالْوَسْوَاسِ الْخَنَّاسِ\n(The whisperer (Al-Waswas) who withdraws.) \"The devil who is squatting (perched) upon the heart of the Son of Adam. So when he becomes absentminded and heedless he whispers. Then, when he remembers Allah he withdraws.\" Mujahid and Qatadah also said this.\nAl-Mu'tamir bin Sulayman reported that his father said, \"It has been mentioned to me that Shaytan is Al-Waswas. He blows into the heart of the Son of Adam when he is sad and when he is happy. But when he (man) remembers Allah, Shaytan withdraws.\" Al-'Awfi reported from Ibn 'Abbas;\nالْوَسْوَاسِ\n(The whisperer.)\n\"He is Shaytan. He whispers and then when he is obeyed, he withdraws.\" As for Allah's saying;\nالَّذِي يُوَسْوِسُ فِي صُدُورِ النَّاسِ\n(Who whispers in the breasts of An-Nas.) Is this specific for the Children of Adam as is apparent, or is it general, including both mankind and Jinns?\nThere are two views concerning this. This is because they (the Jinns) are also included in the usage of the word An-Nas (the people) in most cases.\nIbn Jarir said, \"The phrase Rijalun min Al-Jinn (Men from the Jinns)[referring to Quran 72:6] has been used in reference to them, so it is not strange for the word An-Nas to be applied to them also.\" Then Allah says,\nمِنَ الْجِنَّةِ وَالنَّاسِ\n(Of Jinn and An-Nas.) Is this explanatory of Allah's statement,\nالَّذِي يُوَسْوِسُ فِي صُدُورِ النَّاسِ\n(Who whispers in the breasts of An-Nas.)?\nThen, Allah explains this by saying,\nمِنَ الْجِنَّةِ وَالنَّاسِ\n(Of Jinn and An-Nas.) This is supportive of the second view. It has also been said that Allah's saying,\nمِنَ الْجِنَّةِ وَالنَّاسِ\n(Of Jinn and An-Nas) is an explanation of who is it that whispers into the breasts of mankind from the devils of mankind and Jinns. This is similar to Allah's saying,\nوَكَذَٰلِكَ جَعَلْنَا لِكُلِّ نَبِيٍّ عَدُوًّا شَيَاطِينَ الْإِنسِ وَالْجِنِّ يُوحِي بَعْضُهُمْ إِلَىٰ بَعْضٍ زُخْرُفَ الْقَوْلِ غُرُورًا\n(And so We have appointed for every Prophet enemies – Shayatin among mankind and Jinn, inspiring one another with adorned speech as a delusion.)(6:112)\nImam Ahmad recorded that Ibn 'Abbas said, \"A man came to the Prophet ﷺ and said, 'O Messenger of Allah! Sometimes I say things to myself that I would rather fall from the sky than say (aloud openly).' The Prophet ﷺ said,\nاللهُ أَكْبَرُ، اللهُ أَكْبَرٌ الْحَمْدُ للهِ الَّذِي رَدَّ كَيْدَهُ إِلَى الْوَسْوَسَةِ\n(Allah is Most Great! Allah is Most Great! All praise is due to Allah Who sent his (Shaytan's) plot back as only a whisper.)\"\nAbu Dawud and An-Nasa'i also recorded this Hadith.\nThis is the end of the Tafsir.
